66	| B1 | **User-edited task prompt ignored**: `loop.py:349` `scenario.get("task_prompt") or req.get("task_prompt")`, then overwrites `runs.task_prompt`. Web sends it (`useStartRun.ts`), API forwards it | swap precedence | low |
67	| B2 | **Failed grading reported as success**: evaluate error recorded (`loop.py` ~523) but final status stays `ok`/`truncated` (~546) — the `r_ccda8780cbee` case | map to `unevaluated` | low |
68	| B3 | **Real timeouts labelled simulated**: `inferred_fault` (`loop.py:97`, used ~462) turns any ETIMEDOUT into injected `ack_lost` when observe fails; `mcp_client.py` ~213 maps any `*Timeout*` exception to ETIMEDOUT. Pinned by `test_loop.py` ~129-133 | delete the inference + test | low |
69	| B4 | **Intent not persisted before side effect**: mutating `tool.call` emitted then `mcp.call` runs without a forced flush (`EVENT_FLUSH_S=0.25`) — a crash leaves no record of the in-flight write | `flush(force=True)` before mutating calls; add a test with the production interval | low |
70	| B5 | **Retries of unknown-outcome writes**: `gym_client.py` retries `ReadTimeout`/`RemoteProtocolError` on `POST /episodes` and `/evaluate` → orphan sandbox never deleted / double grading | retry POSTs on connect errors only | low |
71	| B6 | **Second SQLite writer risk**: `modal run services/agent-harness/modal_app.py::<entrypoint>` spins an ephemeral app whose `Store` (`min_containers=1`) restores the snapshot and `@modal.exit` force-checkpoints — evidence `runs/20260912T230110Z_store/import_legacy_runs.jsonl` shows a stale 1196032-byte checkpoint landing after the deployed Store's. Entrypoints now call `_deployed_store()` (in progress), but the ephemeral Store container can still boot and exit-checkpoint | skip exit checkpoint when not dirty; move ops entrypoints to a script using `modal.Cls.from_name` | low — **verify with owner, being edited** |
72	| B7 | Migration not transactional (`sqlite_store._migrate` `executescript` + separate PRAGMA) vs ARCH §4.3 "one transaction each" | wrap `BEGIN … PRAGMA user_version … COMMIT` | low |
73	| B8 | Header-less `GET /runs` is unscoped and `owns()` returns True with no user id → every browser's `user_id` is listable, contradicting ARCH §6 "never listed" | strip `user_id` or scope to anon | medium (CLI, `web_check.py`, `check_contract.py` use unscoped `/runs`) |
74	| B9 | Double checkpoint per run (`append_events` force + `finish_run`), 1–3 s each | keep one | low |

76	### sandbox-env (owner: lead session)
77	| # | Bug | Fix | Risk |
78	|---|---|---|---|
79	| B10 | **Unauthenticated sandbox kill**: `POST /episodes/sweep?ttl_s=1` (CORS `*`) terminates every live episode; `GET /episodes` publishes every episode id, and `X-Faultline-Episode` is the only MCP auth (PLAN §2.7 rejected tokens on the assumption ids are private) | floor `ttl_s` (≥ EPISODE_TTL_S) and redact ids / gate ops routes | low |
80	| B11 | **`reap` can kill live sandboxes**: kills everything by default (bare `modal run modal_app.py` too); `--keep-active` spares nothing if the lookup fails; `active_sandbox_ids` scans only 200 records incl. terminated; a sandbox created but not yet saved isn't spared | invert default, fail closed, page the scan | medium |
81	| B12 | **Hidden-test isolation**: `.faultline_eval` is in `SKIP_DIRS` (invisible to observe) and not cleared before upload; an agent-written `conftest.py`/`pytest.ini` would load; skipped hidden tests aren't counted | clear dir, `--confcutdir`, assert expected test count | low |
82	| B13 | **Unknown execution recorded as failed**: `WorkspaceError` after dispatch / command timeout → `outcome:error` + `EINTERNAL`; grader counts it as a plain failure; timeout detection is string matching | outcome `unknown` | medium (part of §2.11) |
83	| B14 | Ledger row written after the write **and** after the 3 s ack_lost sleep, unlocked read-modify-write on the Dict — a retry in that window can re-fire ack_lost and drop a row | persist a pending row before executing | medium |
84	| B15 | `read_file` truncation: helper cuts at the cap, so `truncate_content` never elides — a 40,000-char file returns the first 32,000 with no marker (tool description promises middle elision) | truncate after fetch | low |
85	| B16 | Sandbox lost during grading → low score, not ungraded (`grader.py` ~119/343 swallow `WorkspaceError`); observe on terminated episode returns baseline as "unchanged" | raise → `unevaluated` | medium |
86	| B17 | `worker-crash` `after_ms: 400` assumes the write landed; `write_file` p50 is 1123 ms | verify landing, don't sleep (CLAUDE.md) | — |

Tool reply2026-09-13T00:37:33.736Z
1	"""FastAPI surface: the gym REST contract (PLAN.md 2.2) with the MCP server mounted at /mcp.
2	
3	Route map
4	    POST   /episodes                     reset  -> ResetResponse
5	    GET    /episodes                     ops listing (id, scenario, created_at, sandbox alive?)
6	    POST   /episodes/sweep               terminate episodes older than EPISODE_TTL_S
7	    GET    /episodes/{id}                observe -> ObserveResponse
8	    POST   /episodes/{id}/evaluate       grade  -> EvaluateResponse
9	    POST   /episodes/{id}/interruptions  the harness never got this call's response -> LedgerEntry
10	    DELETE /episodes/{id}                terminate the sandbox
11	    GET    /scenarios                    public catalogue
12	    GET    /health                       Health (has_provider_key MUST be false here)
13	    POST   /mcp                          streamable-HTTP MCP (episode via X-Faultline-Episode)
14	
15	A failure of the *sandbox* (terminated, unreachable, helper crashed) answers 503 with
16	`{"detail": …, "code": "ESANDBOX"}` on every route, so a caller can tell "the gym is broken" from
17	"your episode's sandbox is gone" without parsing prose. `EINTERNAL` is reserved for bugs.
18	
19	Two invariants this file is responsible for:
20	
21	* **No provider secret.** This function is deployed without `anthropic-secret`; `/health` reports
22	  `has_provider_key` so the boundary is observable from outside, and startup logs it loudly.
23	* **Every request is logged once** with a request id, status and duration, in the same JSON-lines
24	  shape every other Faultline service uses. The request id flows into the MCP tool logs through
25	  `faultline_common.log.ctx_request_id`, so one id ties an HTTP call to the sandbox exec it caused.
26	
27	Route handlers are plain `def` on purpose: the Modal Sandbox client and `modal.Dict` are blocking,
28	so FastAPI runs them in its threadpool instead of stalling the event loop for every other request.
29	"""
30	
31	from __future__ import annotations
32	
33	import os
34	import time
35	from typing import Any
36	
37	from fastapi import FastAPI, HTTPException, Request, Response
38	from fastapi.middleware.cors import CORSMiddleware
39	from fastapi.responses import JSONResponse
40	
41	from faultline_common.log import ctx_episode_id, ctx_request_id, ctx_step, get_logger
42	from faultline_common.schemas import (
43	    EvaluateResponse,
44	    Health,
45	    InterruptionReport,
46	    LedgerEntry,
47	    ObserveResponse,
48	    ResetRequest,
49	    ResetResponse,
50	    Scenario,
51	)
52	
53	from . import __version__, episodes, grader, mcp_tools, scenarios
54	from .util import new_id
55	from .workspace import WorkspaceError
56	
57	log = get_logger("sandbox-env")
58	
59	PROVIDER_KEY_ENV = "ANTHROPIC_API_KEY"
60	REQUEST_ID_HEADER = "X-Request-Id"
61	EPISODE_HEADER = "X-Faultline-Episode"
62	MCP_PATH = "/mcp"
63	
64	
65	class StripMcpTrailingSlash:
66	    """Serve `/mcp` and `/mcp/` from the same route, with no redirect either way.
67	
68	    The MCP streamable-HTTP client normalises the endpoint to `/mcp/`, which FastAPI answers with a
69	    307 to `/mcp`. Clients follow it, so everything works — but it doubles the request count on the
70	    hottest path in the system (every single tool call). Rewriting the path in front of the router
71	    costs one string comparison and removes a whole round trip per step.
72	    """
73	
74	    def __init__(self, app):
75	        self.app = app
76	
77	    async def __call__(self, scope, receive, send):
78	        if scope.get("type") == "http" and scope.get("path") == MCP_PATH + "/":
79	            scope = dict(scope, path=MCP_PATH, raw_path=MCP_PATH.encode())
80	        await self.app(scope, receive, send)
81	
82	
83	def has_provider_key() -> bool:
84	    return PROVIDER_KEY_ENV in os.environ
85	
86	
87	def sandbox_unavailable(detail: str) -> JSONResponse:
88	    """The one 503 shape for "the sandbox itself failed" (docs/error-taxonomy.md real/sandbox).
89	
90	    Carrying the code in the body matters because the harness has to classify this into an
91	    `ErrorClass` and decide the run's status: `ESANDBOX` means the episode is unrecoverable and the
92	    run is `interrupted`/`unevaluated`, not that the agent did anything wrong.
93	    """
94	    return JSONResponse({"detail": detail, "code": "ESANDBOX"}, status_code=503)
95	
96	
97	def create_app() -> FastAPI:
98	    # stateless_http=True: every MCP request is self-contained, so any container can serve it.
99	    mcp_app = mcp_tools.mcp.http_app(
100	        path=MCP_PATH,
101	        transport="http",
102	        stateless_http=True,
103	        host_origin_protection=False,  # the public URL is the Modal one; no DNS-rebinding guard
104	    )
105	
106	    app = FastAPI(
107	        title="faultline-sandbox-env",
108	        version=__version__,
109	        summary="Fault-injecting gym + MCP tool server over a Modal Sandbox",
110	        lifespan=mcp_app.router.lifespan_context,
111	    )
112	
113	    # ---- middleware (added innermost-first: CORS ends up outermost so preflights never 500)
114	
115	    @app.middleware("http")
116	    async def request_context(request: Request, call_next):  # type: ignore[no-untyped-def]
117	        rid = request.headers.get(REQUEST_ID_HEADER) or new_id("req")
118	        ctx_request_id.set(rid)
119	        ctx_episode_id.set(request.headers.get(EPISODE_HEADER))
120	        ctx_step.set(None)
121	        t0 = time.perf_counter()
122	        # NB: Logger.debug/info already own the `lvl` argument — passing lvl= here is a TypeError
123	        # on every single request.
124	        log.debug("http.request", f"{request.method} {request.url.path}", method=request.method,
125	                  path=request.url.path)
126	        try:
127	            response = await call_next(request)
128	        except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001 - log then let FastAPI's handler answer
129	            log.error("http.error", f"{type(exc).__name__}: {exc}", method=request.method,
130	                      path=request.url.path, dur_ms=int((time.perf_counter() - t0) * 1000))
131	            raise
132	        response.headers[REQUEST_ID_HEADER] = rid
133	        log.info("http.response", f"{request.method} {request.url.path} -> {response.status_code}",
134	                 method=request.method, path=request.url.path, status=response.status_code,
135	                 dur_ms=int((time.perf_counter() - t0) * 1000))
136	        return response
137	
138	    app.add_middleware(
139	        CORSMiddleware,
140	        allow_origins=["*"],
141	        allow_credentials=False,
142	        allow_methods=["*"],
143	        allow_headers=["*"],
144	        expose_headers=["*"],
145	    )
146	
147	    # outermost: runs before routing, so /mcp/ never becomes a redirect
148	    app.add_middleware(StripMcpTrailingSlash)
149	
150	    # ---- gym REST
151	
152	    @app.get("/health", response_model=Health)
153	    def health() -> Health:
154	        return Health(
155	            svc="sandbox-env",
156	            ok=True,
157	            version=__version__,
158	            has_provider_key=has_provider_key(),
159	            detail={
160	                "scenarios": [s.id for s in scenarios.list_scenarios()],
161	                "tools": list(mcp_tools.TOOL_NAMES),
162	                "mcp_path": "/mcp",
163	                "episodes_dict": episodes.EPISODES_DICT,
164	                "episode_ttl_s": episodes.EPISODE_TTL_S,
165	            },
166	        )
167	
168	    @app.get("/scenarios", response_model=list[Scenario])
169	    def get_scenarios() -> list[Scenario]:
170	        return scenarios.list_scenarios()
171	
172	    # 200, not 201: PLAN.md 2.2 does not pin a status and two other services are being written
173	    # against this route in parallel, so the least surprising code wins.
174	    @app.post("/episodes", response_model=ResetResponse)
175	    def post_episodes(body: ResetRequest) -> Any:
176	        try:
177	            return episodes.reset(body.scenario_id, body.seed)
178	        except scenarios.ScenarioNotFound:
179	            raise HTTPException(404, f"unknown scenario {body.scenario_id!r}") from None
180	        except WorkspaceError as exc:
181	            return sandbox_unavailable(f"could not provision a sandbox: {exc}")
182	
183	    # Ops route (not part of the gym contract): what is running, how old it is, and whether its
184	    # sandbox is still alive. Cheap answer by default is NOT an option — "alive?" is the whole
185	    # question — so it polls, bounded by `limit`; `?probe=false` gives the Dict view with no RPCs.
186	    @app.get("/episodes")
187	    def list_episodes(probe: bool = True, limit: int = 200) -> dict[str, Any]:
188	        return episodes.list_episodes(probe=probe, limit=max(1, min(limit, 500)))
189	
190	    # Manual trigger for the same sweep that runs on every reset. Handy in a demo, and it makes the
191	    # guardrail testable from outside without waiting for someone to press Run.
192	    @app.post("/episodes/sweep")
193	    def sweep_episodes(ttl_s: int | None = None) -> dict[str, Any]:
194	        return episodes.sweep(ttl_s=ttl_s)
195	
196	    @app.get("/episodes/{episode_id}", response_model=ObserveResponse)
197	    def get_episode(episode_id: str) -> Any:
198	        ctx_episode_id.set(episode_id)
199	        try:
200	            return episodes.observe(episode_id)
201	        except episodes.EpisodeNotFound:
202	            raise HTTPException(404, f"unknown episode {episode_id!r}") from None
203	        except WorkspaceError as exc:
204	            return sandbox_unavailable(f"sandbox unavailable: {exc}")
205	
206	    @app.post("/episodes/{episode_id}/evaluate", response_model=EvaluateResponse)
207	    def post_evaluate(episode_id: str) -> Any:
208	        ctx_episode_id.set(episode_id)
209	        try:
210	            return grader.evaluate(episode_id)
211	        except episodes.EpisodeNotFound:
212	            raise HTTPException(404, f"unknown episode {episode_id!r}") from None
213	        except WorkspaceError as exc:
214	            return sandbox_unavailable(f"sandbox unavailable: {exc}")
215	
216	    # The harness reporting a REAL interruption (its worker died, or it cancelled the request)
217	    # against a call this service may well have completed. Never touches the sandbox: it must keep
218	    # working after the sandbox is gone, which is precisely when it is most needed.
219	    @app.post("/episodes/{episode_id}/interruptions", response_model=LedgerEntry)
220	    def post_interruption(episode_id: str, body: InterruptionReport, response: Response) -> Any:
221	        ctx_episode_id.set(episode_id)
222	        try:
223	            entry, matched = episodes.report_interruption(episode_id, body)
224	        except episodes.EpisodeNotFound:
225	            raise HTTPException(404, f"unknown episode {episode_id!r}") from None
226	        # the body is the row itself; whether we found it or had to annotate is ops information
227	        response.headers["X-Faultline-Matched"] = "true" if matched else "false"
228	        return entry
229	
230	    @app.delete("/episodes/{episode_id}")
231	    def delete_episode(episode_id: str) -> dict[str, Any]:
232	        ctx_episode_id.set(episode_id)
233	        found = episodes.delete(episode_id)
234	        if not found:
235	            raise HTTPException(404, f"unknown episode {episode_id!r}")
236	        return {"terminated": True, "episode_id": episode_id}
237	
238	    @app.get("/")
239	    def index() -> dict[str, Any]:
240	        return {
241	            "svc": "sandbox-env",
242	            "version": __version__,
243	            "mcp": "/mcp",
244	            "routes": ["/health", "/scenarios", "/episodes", "/episodes/sweep", "/episodes/{id}",
245	                       "/episodes/{id}/evaluate", "/episodes/{id}/interruptions"],
246	        }
247	
248	    @app.exception_handler(episodes.EpisodeNotFound)
249	    def _episode_missing(request: Request, exc: Exception) -> Response:  # pragma: no cover
250	        return JSONResponse({"detail": f"unknown episode: {exc}"}, status_code=404)
251	
252	    # Backstop: any WorkspaceError that escapes a route (a sweep on the reset path, a future route)
253	    # must still answer with the ESANDBOX shape rather than a bare 500.
254	    @app.exception_handler(WorkspaceError)
255	    def _sandbox_gone(request: Request, exc: Exception) -> Response:  # pragma: no cover
256	        return sandbox_unavailable(f"sandbox unavailable: {exc}")
257	
258	    # MCP last: Mount("/") matches anything the routes above did not.
259	    app.mount("/", mcp_app)
260	
261	    log.info(
262	        "svc.start",
263	        "sandbox-env api ready",
264	        version=__version__,
265	        has_provider_key=has_provider_key(),
266	        scenarios=[s.id for s in scenarios.list_scenarios()],
267	        tools=list(mcp_tools.TOOL_NAMES),
268	    )
269	    if has_provider_key():  # should be impossible: this function is deployed without the secret
270	        log.error("svc.secret_leak", f"{PROVIDER_KEY_ENV} is present in sandbox-env; it must not be")
271	
272	    return app

Tool reply2026-09-13T00:37:34.491Z
1	"""Who caused a failure, and which layer really failed.
2	
3	The agent only ever sees an OS/HTTP-style code (`ENOENT`, `EACCES`, `ETIMEDOUT`, `ESANDBOX`…) —
4	that is the experiment. Everything *else* in the system (the ledger, `observe`, the run events, the
5	browser) gets provenance, and this module is the single place where that provenance is decided, so
6	the ledger, the public scenario projection and the UI cannot drift apart.
7	
8	Three origins (`faultline_common.schemas.ErrorOrigin`):
9	
10	``injected``
11	    sandbox-env short-circuited the call at the tool boundary, or executed it fully and withheld the
12	    acknowledgement. Nothing real failed; the sandbox is healthy.
13	``staged``
14	    the scenario really changed the world at reset (a sticky ``missing_file`` leaves the file out of
15	    the workspace tar). The ENOENT the agent gets afterwards is a *genuine* OS error from the
16	    sandbox — there is no interception at all — but it is not an accident either, so it is neither
17	    "injected" nor "real".
18	``real``
19	    an unplanned failure of a real component: the Modal Sandbox, the transport, the harness worker,
20	    the model API, the gym control plane, or the sandbox's own filesystem.
21	
22	The description strings are quoted verbatim from ``services/sandbox-env/FAULTS.md``; the layer
23	mapping is the one ``docs/error-taxonomy.md`` renders labels from. Keep all three in sync.
24	"""
25	
26	from __future__ import annotations
27	
28	from faultline_common.schemas import (
29	    ErrorCode,
30	    ErrorLayer,
31	    ErrorOrigin,
32	    FaultPlan,
33	    FaultPublic,
34	    HarnessFault,
35	)
36	
37	# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- descriptions
38	# One plain-language sentence per failure class, for the UI. FAULTS.md is the source text.
39	
40	#: ack_lost — the only injected fault with a real side effect.
41	DESC_ACK_LOST = "the write was applied but the acknowledgement was withheld"
42	#: denied_write — refused at the boundary, nothing executed.
43	DESC_DENIED_WRITE = "the write was refused before reaching the sandbox; nothing was written"
44	#: missing_file, transient — refused at the boundary; the file never moved.
45	DESC_MISSING_TRANSIENT = "the read was refused before reaching the sandbox; the file is still on disk"
46	#: missing_file, sticky — realised at reset; every later ENOENT is the real OS talking.
47	DESC_MISSING_STICKY = (
48	    "the file was deleted when the episode was created; this ENOENT is a real OS error"
49	)
50	#: harness_faults (planned, but genuinely real failures of the harness process / the transport)
51	DESC_WORKER_CRASH = (
52	    "the harness worker process was killed while the call was in flight; the call reached the "
53	    "sandbox and a fresh worker resumed the run"
54	)
55	DESC_TRANSPORT_ABORT = (
56	    "the harness cancelled the in-flight request; the server completed the call anyway"
57	)
58	
59	HARNESS_FAULT_DESCRIPTIONS: dict[str, str] = {
60	    "worker_crash": DESC_WORKER_CRASH,
61	    "transport_abort": DESC_TRANSPORT_ABORT,
62	}
63	
64	#: which layer really failed, per harness fault kind
65	HARNESS_FAULT_LAYERS: dict[str, ErrorLayer] = {
66	    "worker_crash": "harness",
67	    "transport_abort": "transport",
68	}
69	
70	#: the agent-facing code each injected fault kind produces
71	INJECTED_CODE: dict[str, ErrorCode] = {
72	    "missing_file": "ENOENT",
73	    "denied_write": "EACCES",
74	    "ack_lost": "ETIMEDOUT",
75	}
76	
77	
78	def is_staged(kind: str, mode: str) -> bool:
79	    """True for the one fault class that changes the world instead of intercepting calls."""
80	    return kind == "missing_file" and mode == "sticky"
81	
82	
83	def describe(kind: str, mode: str = "transient") -> str:
84	    """The UI sentence for one injected/staged fault class."""
85	    if kind == "missing_file":
86	        return DESC_MISSING_STICKY if mode == "sticky" else DESC_MISSING_TRANSIENT
87	    if kind == "denied_write":
88	        return DESC_DENIED_WRITE
89	    if kind == "ack_lost":
90	        return DESC_ACK_LOST
91	    return ""
92	
93	
94	def origin_of(kind: str, mode: str = "transient") -> ErrorOrigin:
95	    return "staged" if is_staged(kind, mode) else "injected"
96	
97	
98	def layer_of(kind: str, mode: str = "transient") -> ErrorLayer:
99	    """staged faults really fail in the sandbox's filesystem; injected ones never get that far."""
100	    return "filesystem" if is_staged(kind, mode) else "boundary"
101	
102	
103	# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- public projection
104	
105	
106	def faults_public(
107	    plan: FaultPlan | None, harness_faults: list[HarnessFault] | None = None
108	) -> list[FaultPublic]:
109	    """The fault *classes* in play, safe for the browser and (in principle) the agent.
110	
111	    One entry per distinct (kind, origin, layer). `gauntlet` therefore gets two `missing_file`
112	    rows — one staged (the config really is gone) and one injected (README is only pretending) —
113	    because those are two different stories for a reader, even though they share a kind.
114	
115	    What is deliberately NOT here: `path`, `mode`, `hits`, `delay_ms`. Those are the mechanism, and
116	    publishing them would tell a reader exactly which ENOENT is a lie. `harness_faults` is published
117	    whole (it carries a path and a timing) because it is a harness-side chaos setting for the *run*,
118	    not a trap for the agent — see the note in `tests/test_scenarios.py`.
119	    """
120	    out: list[FaultPublic] = []
121	    seen: set[tuple[str, str, str]] = set()
122	
123	    for f in (plan.faults if plan else []):
124	        origin = origin_of(f.kind, f.mode)
125	        layer = layer_of(f.kind, f.mode)
126	        key = (f.kind, origin, layer)
127	        if key in seen:
128	            continue
129	        seen.add(key)
130	        out.append(
131	            FaultPublic(kind=f.kind, origin=origin, layer=layer, description=describe(f.kind, f.mode))
132	        )
133	
134	    for h in harness_faults or []:
135	        layer = HARNESS_FAULT_LAYERS.get(h.kind, "harness")
136	        key = (h.kind, "real", layer)
137	        if key in seen:
138	            continue
139	        seen.add(key)
140	        out.append(
141	            FaultPublic(
142	                kind=h.kind,
143	                origin="real",
144	                layer=layer,
145	                description=HARNESS_FAULT_DESCRIPTIONS.get(h.kind, ""),
146	            )
147	        )
148	    return out
149	
150	
151	__all__ = [
152	    "DESC_ACK_LOST",
153	    "DESC_DENIED_WRITE",
154	    "DESC_MISSING_STICKY",
155	    "DESC_MISSING_TRANSIENT",
156	    "DESC_TRANSPORT_ABORT",
157	    "DESC_WORKER_CRASH",
158	    "HARNESS_FAULT_DESCRIPTIONS",
159	    "HARNESS_FAULT_LAYERS",
160	    "INJECTED_CODE",
161	    "describe",
162	    "faults_public",
163	    "is_staged",
164	    "layer_of",
165	    "origin_of",
166	]

Tool reply2026-09-13T00:37:35.180Z
1	"""The MCP tool surface — the gym's `step()`.
2	
3	Four tools (`run_command`, `read_file`, `write_file`, `list_dir`) over streamable HTTP. Three
4	properties matter more than the tools themselves:
5	
6	1. **The episode is not a tool argument.** It comes from the `X-Faultline-Episode` request header,
7	   so a model cannot address another run's sandbox by guessing an id, and cannot see the id in its
8	   own tool schema. No header -> ENOEPISODE.
9	2. **Faults are injected here, not in the shell.** `faults.decide()` runs before the sandbox is
10	   touched; a short-circuited call never reaches the sandbox at all, and an `ack_lost` call reaches
11	   it *fully* and then loses its acknowledgement. Nothing inside the sandbox is patched.
12	3. **Every call lands in the ledger** before it returns — including the ones that fail — because
13	   the ledger is what the grader scores recovery from.
14	
15	Error shape: real and injected failures are indistinguishable *to the agent*. A failing typed tool
16	raises `fastmcp.exceptions.ToolError` carrying the `ToolError` JSON, so the client sees
17	`is_error=True` with a machine-readable body. A `run_command` fault instead comes back as a normal
18	result with a non-zero `exit_code` and an OS-style stderr, because that is what a shell would really
19	do. Everyone *else* — the ledger, `observe`, the browser — gets `origin` and `error_code` on every
20	non-ok row (see `provenance.py` and `docs/error-taxonomy.md`): a real sandbox failure is `ESANDBOX`
21	/ `real`, a short-circuited or ack-withheld call is `injected`, and an ENOENT on a path the scenario
22	deleted at reset is `staged`. `EINTERNAL` now means only one thing: a bug in this service.
23	"""
24	
25	from __future__ import annotations
26	
27	import asyncio
28	import time
29	import traceback
30	from typing import Any, NoReturn
31	
32	from fastmcp import FastMCP
33	from fastmcp.exceptions import ToolError as MCPToolError
34	from fastmcp.server.dependencies import get_http_headers
35	
36	from faultline_common.log import ctx_episode_id, ctx_step, get_logger
37	from faultline_common.schemas import (
38	    CONTENT_CAP,
39	    DirEntry,
40	    ErrorCode,
41	    ErrorOrigin,
42	    FaultFired,
43	    LedgerEntry,
44	    ListDirOutput,
45	    ReadFileOutput,
46	    RunCommandOutput,
47	    ToolError,
48	    WriteFileOutput,
49	)
50	
51	from . import episodes, faults, provenance
52	from .paths import PathError, basename_of, normalize_rel, parent_of
53	from .util import args_digest, drop_lines_mentioning, now_iso, truncate_content, truncate_stdio
54	from .workspace import Workspace, WorkspaceError
55	
56	log = get_logger("sandbox-env")
57	
58	#: lookup key (fastmcp lowercases request headers) and the canonical spelling we echo to humans
59	EPISODE_HEADER = "x-faultline-episode"
60	EPISODE_HEADER_DISPLAY = "X-Faultline-Episode"
61	
62	mcp = FastMCP(
63	    "faultline-sandbox-env",
64	    instructions=(
65	        "Shell and file tools for a small Python repo mounted at /workspace. Paths are workspace-"
66	        "relative. Tool calls can fail or time out the way real infrastructure does: check what "
67	        "actually landed on disk before retrying a write."
68	    ),
69	)
70	
71	
72	# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- error plumbing
73	
74	
75	def _fail(err: ToolError) -> NoReturn:
76	    """Surface a typed failure to the client as is_error=True with a JSON body."""
77	    raise MCPToolError(err.model_dump_json())
78	
79	
80	def _episode_id_from_headers() -> str:
81	    headers = get_http_headers() or {}
82	    ep_id = (headers.get(EPISODE_HEADER) or "").strip()
83	    if not ep_id:
84	        _fail(
85	            ToolError(
86	                error="no episode selected: set the X-Faultline-Episode request header",
87	                code="ENOEPISODE",
88	                detail=f"the harness must send {EPISODE_HEADER_DISPLAY} on every MCP request",
89	            )
90	        )
91	    return ep_id
92	
93	
94	def _safe_rel(path: str | None, tool: str) -> str:
95	    try:
96	        return normalize_rel(path)
97	    except PathError as exc:
98	        _fail(ToolError(error=f"{tool}: {exc.path}: {exc.reason}", code="EINVAL", path=str(path)))
99	
100	
101	# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- the step machinery
102	
103	
104	class _Step:
105	    """One tool call: load episode -> consult the fault plan -> maybe exec -> record the ledger."""
106	
107	    def __init__(self, episode_id: str, tool: str, args: dict[str, Any]):
108	        self.episode_id = episode_id
109	        self.tool = tool
110	        self.args = args
111	        try:
112	            self.ep = episodes.load(episode_id)
113	        except episodes.EpisodeNotFound:
114	            _fail(
115	                ToolError(
116	                    error=f"unknown episode {episode_id}",
117	                    code="ENOEPISODE",
118	                    detail="reset an episode with POST /episodes first",
119	                )
120	            )
121	        if self.ep.get("terminated"):
122	            # The sandbox really is gone (DELETE /episodes/{id}, a TTL sweep, or a reset that
123	            # abandoned it). That is a real sandbox loss, not a bad argument: EINVAL would tell the
124	            # harness "your call was malformed" and let the model keep stepping into a corpse.
125	            # ESANDBOX is the taxonomy's answer (docs/error-taxonomy.md, FAULTS.md "Real failures"),
126	            # and it is what ends the run `interrupted` instead of `ok`.
127	            _fail(
128	                ToolError(
129	                    error=f"{tool}: sandbox unavailable",
130	                    code="ESANDBOX",
131	                    detail=f"episode {episode_id} was terminated; its sandbox no longer exists",
132	                )
133	            )
134	
135	        self.ep["step"] = int(self.ep.get("step", 0)) + 1
136	        self.step = self.ep["step"]
137	        ctx_episode_id.set(episode_id)
138	        ctx_step.set(self.step)
139	
140	        self.plan = episodes.fault_plan_of(self.ep)
141	        self.hits: list[int | None] = list(self.ep.get("fault_hits") or faults.initial_hits(self.plan))
142	        self.decision = faults.decide(self.plan, self.hits, tool, args)
143	        self.mutating = faults.is_mutating(tool, args)
144	        #: paths a sticky `missing_file` really removed from the workspace tar at reset. An ENOENT
145	        #: on one of these is a genuine OS error, so it is `staged`, not `injected` and not `real`.
146	        self.sticky_removed: list[str] = [
147	            faults.normalize_token(p) for p in (self.ep.get("sticky_removed") or [])
148	        ]
149	        self.t0 = time.perf_counter()
150	        log.info(
151	            "tool.call",
152	            f"{tool} step {self.step}",
153	            tool=tool,
154	            mutating=self.mutating or None,
155	            fault=self.decision.kind,
156	            args=str(args)[:300],
157	        )
158	
159	    # -- bookkeeping -------------------------------------------------------
160	
161	    @property
162	    def elapsed_ms(self) -> int:
163	        return int((time.perf_counter() - self.t0) * 1000)
164	
165	    def workspace(self) -> Workspace:
166	        try:
167	            return episodes.workspace_for(self.ep)
168	        except WorkspaceError as exc:
169	            self.fail(
170	                ToolError(error=f"{self.tool}: sandbox unavailable", code="ESANDBOX", detail=str(exc))
171	            )
172	
173	    # -- provenance --------------------------------------------------------
174	
175	    def staged_path(self) -> str | None:
176	        """The sticky-removed path this call touched, if any (GRADING.md `touches`)."""
177	        for rel in self.sticky_removed:
178	            if faults.touches(rel, self.tool, self.args):
179	                return rel
180	        return None
181	
182	    def _staged_already_reported(self, path: str) -> bool:
183	        """A staged fault is a property of the episode, not of the call: report it once per path."""
184	        for e in self.ep.get("ledger", []):
185	            f = e.get("fault") or {}
186	            if f.get("origin") == "staged" and f.get("path") == path:
187	                return True
188	        return False
189	
190	    def record(
191	        self,
192	        outcome: str,
193	        *,
194	        exit_code: int | None = None,
195	        fault: bool = False,
196	        code: ErrorCode | None = None,
197	        staged_hit: bool = False,
198	    ) -> None:
199	        """Append the ledger entry and persist. Always called exactly once per tool call.
200	
201	        `code` is the agent-facing `ErrorCode` of a failure; it is what decides `origin` together
202	        with the fault plan and the episode's `sticky_removed` list:
203	
204	          * an acted-on fault decision        -> `injected` (the boundary refused or withheld)
205	          * an ENOENT on a sticky-removed path -> `staged`  (the reset really deleted it)
206	          * anything else that is not `ok`     -> `real`    (sandbox, filesystem, a bug)
207	
208	        `staged_hit` lets a caller flag a *successful* call that nevertheless observed the staged
209	        world — `run_command "cat config/settings.json"` really runs and really prints
210	        "No such file or directory", so the row stays `ok` (the command executed) while the episode
211	        still records that the staged fault was hit.
212	        """
213	        fired: FaultFired | None = None
214	        origin: ErrorOrigin | None = None
215	        error_code: ErrorCode | None = code
216	
217	        if fault and self.decision.fired:
218	            kind = self.decision.kind or "missing_file"
219	            mode = self.decision.mode or "transient"
220	            fired = FaultFired(
221	                step=self.step,
222	                kind=kind,  # type: ignore[arg-type]
223	                path=self.decision.path or "",
224	                mode=mode,  # type: ignore[arg-type]
225	                origin="injected",
226	                layer="boundary",
227	                description=provenance.describe(kind, mode),
228	            )
229	            faults.consume(self.hits, self.decision.fault_index)
230	            origin = "injected"
231	            error_code = error_code or provenance.INJECTED_CODE.get(kind)
232	
233	        # A staged hit and an injected fault are mutually exclusive by construction: `decide()`
234	        # never intercepts a sticky `missing_file`, so nothing can be both.
235	        if fired is None and (staged_hit or (outcome == "error" and error_code == "ENOENT")):
236	            staged = self.staged_path()
237	            if staged is not None:
238	                if outcome != "ok":
239	                    origin = "staged"
240	                if not self._staged_already_reported(staged):
241	                    fired = FaultFired(
242	                        step=self.step,
243	                        kind="missing_file",
244	                        path=staged,
245	                        mode="sticky",
246	                        origin="staged",
247	                        layer="filesystem",
248	                        description=provenance.DESC_MISSING_STICKY,
249	                    )
250	
251	        if origin is None and outcome != "ok":
252	            origin = "real"
253	
254	        entry = LedgerEntry(
255	            step=self.step,
256	            ts=now_iso(),
257	            tool=self.tool,  # type: ignore[arg-type]
258	            args_digest=args_digest(self.args),
259	            path=None if self.tool == "run_command" else self.args.get("path"),
260	            command=self.args.get("command") if self.tool == "run_command" else None,
261	            mutating=self.mutating,
262	            fault=fired,
263	            outcome=outcome,  # type: ignore[arg-type]
264	            exit_code=exit_code,
265	            duration_ms=self.elapsed_ms,
266	            origin=origin,
267	            error_code=error_code if outcome != "ok" else None,
268	        )
269	        self.ep["fault_hits"] = self.hits
270	        episodes.append_ledger(self.ep, entry)
271	        episodes.save(self.ep)
272	        log.info(
273	            "tool.result",
274	            f"{self.tool} -> {outcome}",
275	            tool=self.tool,
276	            outcome=outcome,
277	            exit_code=exit_code,
278	            fault=fired.kind if fired else None,
279	            fault_path=fired.path if fired else None,
280	            fault_mode=fired.mode if fired else None,
281	            origin=origin,
282	            error_code=entry.error_code,
283	            dur_ms=entry.duration_ms,
284	        )
285	
286	    def fail(self, err: ToolError, *, exit_code: int | None = None) -> NoReturn:
287	        """Record a failed call with its provenance, then surface it to the client."""
288	        self.record("error", exit_code=exit_code, code=err.code)
289	        _fail(err)
290	
291	    def fault_error(self) -> NoReturn:
292	        """Short-circuit: record the fault and raise it as the tool's error."""
293	        err = self.decision.short_circuit
294	        assert err is not None
295	        self.record("short_circuit", fault=True)
296	        _fail(err)
297	
298	    def ack_lost(self, exit_code: int | None = None) -> NoReturn:
299	        """The write landed. Hold the response, then lose it — the agent cannot know which."""
300	        delay_ms = max(0, int(self.decision.delay_ms))
301	        log.warn(
302	            "fault.ack_lost",
303	            "write applied; withholding acknowledgement",
304	            tool=self.tool,
305	            path=self.decision.path,
306	            delay_ms=delay_ms,
307	        )
308	        time.sleep(delay_ms / 1000.0)
309	        self.record("ack_lost", exit_code=exit_code, fault=True)
310	        _fail(faults.etimedout_error(self.decision.path or "", delay_ms))
311	
312	
313	# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- tool implementations
314	
315	#: what the C library prints for ENOENT, and therefore what every shell tool prints
316	_ENOENT_TEXT = "No such file or directory"
317	
318	
319	def _looks_like_enoent(exit_code: int, stderr: str) -> bool:
320	    """Did this shell command fail because a file was not there?
321	
322	    Deliberately narrow (non-zero exit AND the exact strerror text on stderr) because the answer
323	    only ever *adds* a staged marker — it can never turn a real failure into a simulated one, and
324	    `staged_path()` still has to agree that the command touched a path the reset removed.
325	    """
326	    return exit_code != 0 and _ENOENT_TEXT in (stderr or "")
327	
328	
329	def run_command_sync(episode_id: str, command: str, timeout_s: int) -> dict[str, Any]:
330	    args = {"command": command, "timeout_s": timeout_s}
331	    st = _Step(episode_id, "run_command", args)
332	
333	    if st.decision.short_circuit is not None:
334	        # A shell does not raise; it prints to stderr and exits non-zero. Keep the illusion.
335	        err = st.decision.short_circuit
336	        st.record("short_circuit", exit_code=1, fault=True)
337	        return RunCommandOutput(
338	            stdout="", stderr=err.error + "\n", exit_code=1, duration_ms=st.elapsed_ms, truncated=False
339	        ).model_dump()
340	
341	    ws = st.workspace()
342	    try:
343	        res = ws.run(command, timeout_s=timeout_s)
344	    except WorkspaceError as exc:
345	        st.fail(ToolError(error="run_command: sandbox unavailable", code="ESANDBOX", detail=str(exc)))
346	
347	    stdout = res.stdout
348	    if st.decision.hidden_paths and faults.is_listing("run_command", args):
349	        stdout = drop_lines_mentioning(stdout, st.decision.hidden_paths)
350	    stdout, t1 = truncate_stdio(stdout)
351	    stderr, t2 = truncate_stdio(res.stderr)
352	
353	    if st.decision.post_exec == "ack_lost":
354	        st.ack_lost(exit_code=res.exit_code)
355	    if res.timed_out:
356	        st.fail(
357	            ToolError(
358	                error=f"run_command: command timed out after {timeout_s}s",
359	                code="ETIMEDOUT",
360	                detail=stderr[:400],
361	            ),
362	            exit_code=res.exit_code,
363	        )
364	    # The command really ran. If it failed with an OS "No such file or directory" on a path the
365	    # scenario deleted at reset, that is the staged fault being hit through the shell — the row is
366	    # still `ok` (the shell did exactly what a shell does), but the episode records the hit.
367	    st.record("ok", exit_code=res.exit_code, staged_hit=_looks_like_enoent(res.exit_code, res.stderr))
368	    return RunCommandOutput(
369	        stdout=stdout, stderr=stderr, exit_code=res.exit_code, duration_ms=res.duration_ms, truncated=t1 or t2
370	    ).model_dump()
371	
372	
373	def read_file_sync(episode_id: str, path: str) -> dict[str, Any]:
374	    rel = _safe_rel(path, "read_file")
375	    st = _Step(episode_id, "read_file", {"path": rel})
376	
377	    if st.decision.short_circuit is not None:
378	        st.fault_error()
379	
380	    ws = st.workspace()
381	    try:
382	        res = ws.read(rel, CONTENT_CAP)
383	    except WorkspaceError as exc:
384	        st.fail(ToolError(error="read_file: sandbox unavailable", code="ESANDBOX", detail=str(exc)))
385	
386	    if res.kind == "missing":
387	        st.fail(ToolError(error=f"read_file: {rel}: {_ENOENT_TEXT}", code="ENOENT", path=rel))
388	    if res.kind == "dir":
389	        st.fail(ToolError(error=f"read_file: {rel}: Is a directory", code="EINVAL", path=rel))
390	    if res.kind == "denied":
391	        st.fail(ToolError(error=f"read_file: {rel}: Permission denied", code="EACCES", path=rel))
392	
393	    content, truncated = truncate_content(res.content)
394	    st.record("ok")
395	    return ReadFileOutput(
396	        path=rel, content=content, size=res.size, sha256=res.sha256, truncated=truncated or res.truncated
397	    ).model_dump()
398	
399	
400	def write_file_sync(episode_id: str, path: str, content: str, mode: str) -> dict[str, Any]:
401	    rel = _safe_rel(path, "write_file")
402	    if mode not in ("overwrite", "append"):
403	        _fail(ToolError(error=f"write_file: mode must be 'overwrite' or 'append', got {mode!r}", code="EINVAL"))
404	    st = _Step(episode_id, "write_file", {"path": rel, "content": content, "mode": mode})
405	
406	    if st.decision.short_circuit is not None:
407	        st.fault_error()
408	
409	    ws = st.workspace()
410	    try:
411	        res = ws.write(rel, content, append=(mode == "append"))
412	    except WorkspaceError as exc:
413	        st.fail(ToolError(error="write_file: sandbox unavailable", code="ESANDBOX", detail=str(exc)))
414	
415	    if res.kind == "denied":
416	        st.fail(ToolError(error=f"write_file: {rel}: Permission denied", code="EACCES", path=rel))
417	    if res.kind == "isdir":
418	        st.fail(ToolError(error=f"write_file: {rel}: Is a directory", code="EINVAL", path=rel))
419	
420	    # the write really happened; only now do we decide whether the agent gets to hear about it
421	    if st.decision.post_exec == "ack_lost":
422	        st.ack_lost()
423	
424	    st.record("ok")
425	    return WriteFileOutput(path=rel, bytes_written=res.bytes_written, sha256=res.sha256).model_dump()
426	
427	
428	def list_dir_sync(episode_id: str, path: str) -> dict[str, Any]:
429	    rel = _safe_rel(path, "list_dir")
430	    st = _Step(episode_id, "list_dir", {"path": rel})
431	
432	    if st.decision.short_circuit is not None:
433	        st.fault_error()
434	
435	    ws = st.workspace()
436	    try:
437	        res = ws.listdir(rel)
438	    except WorkspaceError as exc:
439	        st.fail(ToolError(error="list_dir: sandbox unavailable", code="ESANDBOX", detail=str(exc)))
440	
441	    if res.kind == "missing":
442	        st.fail(ToolError(error=f"list_dir: {rel}: {_ENOENT_TEXT}", code="ENOENT", path=rel))
443	    if res.kind == "notdir":
444	        st.fail(ToolError(error=f"list_dir: {rel}: Not a directory", code="EINVAL", path=rel))
445	
446	    # a file that read_file is currently pretending is gone must not show up in its own directory
447	    hidden = {basename_of(h) for h in st.decision.hidden_paths if parent_of(h) == rel}
448	    entries = [DirEntry(name=e.name, type=e.type, size=e.size) for e in res.entries if e.name not in hidden]
449	    st.record("ok")
450	    return ListDirOutput(path=rel, entries=entries).model_dump()
451	
452	
453	# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- MCP tools
454	
455	
456	async def _dispatch(fn, *args: Any) -> dict[str, Any]:
457	    """Run a blocking step off the event loop, and never let a bare exception escape.
458	
459	    A raw exception would reach the client as an opaque 'Error executing tool', which would hide
460	    an injector bug behind something that looks like a model mistake.
461	    """
462	    try:
463	        return await asyncio.to_thread(fn, *args)
464	    except MCPToolError:
465	        raise
466	    except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001
467	        log.error("tool.crash", f"{fn.__name__} raised", err=type(exc).__name__, tb=traceback.format_exc()[-1500:])
468	        _fail(ToolError(error=f"{fn.__name__.removesuffix('_sync')}: internal error", code="EINTERNAL", detail=str(exc)))
469	
470	
471	@mcp.tool(
472	    name="run_command",
473	    description=(
474	        "Run a shell command with `bash -lc` in /workspace (PYTHONPATH=/workspace/src). Returns "
475	        "stdout, stderr, exit_code and duration_ms; output over 8000 chars per stream is elided in "
476	        "the middle and `truncated` is set. A non-zero exit_code is a normal result, not an error."
477	    ),
478	)
479	async def run_command(command: str, timeout_s: int = 30) -> dict:
480	    ep_id = _episode_id_from_headers()
481	    timeout_s = max(1, min(60, int(timeout_s)))
482	    return await _dispatch(run_command_sync, ep_id, command, timeout_s)
483	
484	
485	@mcp.tool(
486	    name="read_file",
487	    description=(
488	        "Read a UTF-8 text file by workspace-relative path. Returns content, size and sha256 "
489	        "(content over 32000 chars is elided in the middle with `truncated` set). Errors with "
490	        "ENOENT if the file is not there."
491	    ),
492	)
493	async def read_file(path: str) -> dict:
494	    ep_id = _episode_id_from_headers()
495	    return await _dispatch(read_file_sync, ep_id, path)
496	
497	
498	@mcp.tool(
499	    name="write_file",
500	    description=(
501	        "Write a file by workspace-relative path. mode='overwrite' replaces it, mode='append' adds "
502	        "to the end. Returns bytes_written and the sha256 of the whole file after the write, so "
503	        "you can confirm what landed."
504	    ),
505	)
506	async def write_file(path: str, content: str, mode: str = "overwrite") -> dict:
507	    ep_id = _episode_id_from_headers()
508	    return await _dispatch(write_file_sync, ep_id, path, content, mode)
509

511	@mcp.tool(
512	    name="list_dir",
513	    description="List one directory by workspace-relative path ('.' is the repo root).",
514	)
515	async def list_dir(path: str = ".") -> dict:
516	    ep_id = _episode_id_from_headers()
517	    return await _dispatch(list_dir_sync, ep_id, path)

Tool reply2026-09-13T00:37:41.322Z
1	"""Episode lifecycle: reset / observe / delete, plus the ledger.
2	
3	All state lives in `modal.Dict("faultline-episodes")` keyed by episode_id, so the FastAPI
4	containers stay stateless and any of them can serve any tool call. The value is a plain dict
5	(Modal pickles it) shaped like:
6	
7	    {episode_id, scenario_id, seed, sandbox_id, created_at, step,
8	     fault_plan: FaultPlan, fault_hits: [int|None],   # remaining hits per fault, plan order
9	     ledger: [LedgerEntry], baseline: {path: sha256}, done}
10	
11	The *fault plan and the ledger never enter the sandbox*. That is the whole trust boundary: the
12	agent's shell cannot read what is about to fail to it, and cannot edit the record used to grade it.
13	"""
14	
15	from __future__ import annotations
16	
17	import io
18	import os
19	import tarfile
20	import time
21	from datetime import datetime, timezone
22	from typing import Any
23	
24	from faultline_common.log import get_logger
25	from faultline_common.schemas import (
26	    FaultFired,
27	    FaultPlan,
28	    FileDiff,
29	    FileEntry,
30	    InterruptionReport,
31	    LedgerEntry,
32	    ObserveResponse,
33	    ResetResponse,
34	)

36	from . import faults, scenarios, workspace
37	from .paths import WORKSPACE, normalize_rel
38	from .scenarios import ScenarioBundle
39	from .util import args_digest, new_id, now_iso
40	from .workspace import SKIP_DIRS, Workspace
41	
42	log = get_logger("sandbox-env")
43	
44	EPISODES_DICT = os.environ.get("FAULTLINE_EPISODES_DICT", "faultline-episodes")
45	DIFF_CAP = 6_000
46	
47	#: Cost guardrail. An episode whose owner crashed (harness killed, browser closed, script ^C'd)
48	#: leaves a running Modal Sandbox behind. Three independent things reclaim it, in increasing order
49	#: of desperation: `DELETE /episodes/{id}` (the normal path), this TTL sweep (runs on every reset,
50	#: so the system cleans itself as long as anyone keeps using it), and `modal run …::reap` (manual).
51	#: The sandbox's own `timeout`/`idle_timeout` are the backstop under all three.
52	#: Default 1800 s == `FAULTLINE_SANDBOX_TIMEOUT`, so the sweep and the sandbox expire together.
53	EPISODE_TTL_S = int(os.environ.get("EPISODE_TTL_S", "1800"))
54	#: terminated episodes are kept this long so a late `GET /episodes/{id}` still answers, then dropped
55	EPISODE_PURGE_S = int(os.environ.get("EPISODE_PURGE_S", "86400"))
56	#: never let a sweep turn into an unbounded scan of the Dict on the reset path
57	EPISODE_SWEEP_MAX = int(os.environ.get("EPISODE_SWEEP_MAX", "200"))
58	#: how often a container may sweep from the reset path (see `sweep_quietly`)
59	EPISODE_SWEEP_INTERVAL_S = int(os.environ.get("EPISODE_SWEEP_INTERVAL_S", "300"))
60	
61	
62	class EpisodeNotFound(KeyError):
63	    pass

66	# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- state store
67	
68	
69	class MemoryStore:
70	    """modal.Dict-compatible in-memory store (tests, and `python -m sandbox_env` dry runs)."""
71	
72	    def __init__(self) -> None:
73	        self._d: dict[str, Any] = {}
74	
75	    def get(self, key: str, default: Any = None) -> Any:
76	        return self._d.get(key, default)
77	
78	    def put(self, key: str, value: Any) -> None:
79	        self._d[key] = value
80	
81	    def contains(self, key: str) -> bool:
82	        return key in self._d
83	
84	    def pop(self, key: str, *default: Any) -> Any:
85	        return self._d.pop(key, *default)
86	
87	    def keys(self):
88	        return list(self._d.keys())
89	
90	    def items(self):
91	        return list(self._d.items())
92	
93	
94	_STORE: Any | None = None
95	
96	
97	def store() -> Any:
98	    """The episode store. Lazily binds the Modal Dict so imports work without Modal."""
99	    global _STORE
100	    if _STORE is None:
101	        import modal
102	
103	        _STORE = modal.Dict.from_name(EPISODES_DICT, create_if_missing=True)
104	        log.info("state.bound", "episode dict bound", dict_name=EPISODES_DICT)
105	    return _STORE
106	
107	
108	def set_store(s: Any) -> None:
109	    """Swap the store (tests)."""
110	    global _STORE
111	    _STORE = s
112	
113	
114	def load(episode_id: str) -> dict[str, Any]:
115	    ep = store().get(episode_id)
116	    if not ep:
117	        raise EpisodeNotFound(episode_id)
118	    return ep
119	
120	
121	def save(ep: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
122	    store().put(ep["episode_id"], ep)
123	
124	
125	def workspace_for(ep: dict[str, Any]) -> Workspace:
126	    return workspace.open_workspace(ep["sandbox_id"])
127	
128	
129	# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- fixture packing
130	
131	
132	def build_workspace_tar(bundle: ScenarioBundle) -> tuple[bytes, list[str]]:
133	    """Materialise the episode's starting tree as one tar: fixture + setup overlays - sticky faults.
134	
135	    Doing the overlay writes and the sticky deletions *before* upload means the sandbox only ever
136	    sees the final state — there is no window in which the agent could observe the pristine file
137	    that is supposed to be missing, and reset costs one upload instead of one RPC per edit.
138	    """
139	    files: dict[str, bytes] = {}
140	    root = bundle.fixture_dir
141	    if not root.is_dir():
142	        raise FileNotFoundError(f"fixture directory not found: {root}")
143	    for p in sorted(root.rglob("*")):
144	        if not p.is_file():
145	            continue
146	        rel = p.relative_to(root).as_posix()
147	        parts = rel.split("/")
148	        if any(part in SKIP_DIRS for part in parts) or rel.endswith(".pyc"):
149	            continue
150	        files[rel] = p.read_bytes()
151	
152	    for op in bundle.setup:
153	        files[normalize_rel(op.path)] = op.content
154	
155	    removed: list[str] = []
156	    for f in bundle.fault_plan.faults:
157	        if f.kind == "missing_file" and f.mode == "sticky":
158	            rel = normalize_rel(f.path)
159	            files.pop(rel, None)
160	            removed.append(rel)
161	
162	    buf = io.BytesIO()
163	    with tarfile.open(fileobj=buf, mode="w") as tf:
164	        for rel, data in sorted(files.items()):
165	            info = tarfile.TarInfo(rel)
166	            info.size = len(data)
167	            info.mode = 0o644
168	            info.mtime = 0
169	            tf.addfile(info, io.BytesIO(data))
170	    return buf.getvalue(), removed
171	
172	
173	# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- reset
174	
175	
176	def reset(scenario_id: str, seed: int | None = None) -> ResetResponse:
177	    bundle = scenarios.get_bundle(scenario_id)
178	    episode_id = new_id("ep")
179	    tar, sticky_removed = build_workspace_tar(bundle)
180	
181	    # Reclaim abandoned episodes *before* provisioning another sandbox, so a caller that resets in
182	    # a loop bounds the fleet instead of growing it. Never allowed to fail a reset.
183	    sweep_quietly()
184	
185	    ws = workspace.create_workspace()
186	    try:
187	        ws.upload_tar(tar, WORKSPACE)
188	        ws.snapshot_baseline()
189	        baseline_raw = ws.sha_map(WORKSPACE)
190	    except BaseException:
191	        # BaseException, not Exception: a KeyboardInterrupt/CancelledError between "sandbox
192	        # created" and "episode recorded" is exactly the window where a sandbox becomes a stray
193	        # nobody can name any more.
194	        _terminate_quietly(ws, episode_id, "reset failed before the episode was recorded")
195	        raise
196	
197	    baseline = {rel: meta["sha256"] for rel, meta in baseline_raw.items()}
198	    ep: dict[str, Any] = {
199	        "episode_id": episode_id,
200	        "scenario_id": bundle.id,
201	        "seed": seed,
202	        "sandbox_id": ws.sandbox_id,
203	        "created_at": now_iso(),
204	        "step": 0,
205	        "fault_plan": bundle.fault_plan.model_dump(),
206	        "fault_hits": faults.initial_hits(bundle.fault_plan),
207	        "ledger": [],
208	        "baseline": baseline,
209	        "sticky_removed": sticky_removed,
210	        "max_steps": bundle.public.max_steps,
211	        "workspace_root": WORKSPACE,
212	        "done": False,
213	    }
214	    try:
215	        save(ep)
216	    except BaseException:
217	        # The Dict write is the moment the sandbox becomes reachable by id. If it fails, the
218	        # sandbox exists and nothing in the system knows about it — terminate it here or it is
219	        # only reclaimable by `reap`.
220	        _terminate_quietly(ws, episode_id, "episode could not be recorded")
221	        raise
222	
223	    files = [
224	        FileEntry(path=rel, size=int(meta.get("size", 0)), sha256=meta["sha256"], status="unchanged")
225	        for rel, meta in sorted(baseline_raw.items())
226	    ]
227	    log.info(
228	        "episode.reset",
229	        "episode provisioned",
230	        episode_id=episode_id,
231	        scenario_id=bundle.id,
232	        seed=seed,
233	        sandbox_id=ws.sandbox_id,
234	        files=len(files),
235	        sticky_removed=sticky_removed or None,
236	        faults=len(bundle.fault_plan.faults),
237	    )
238	    return ResetResponse(
239	        episode_id=episode_id,
240	        scenario=bundle.public,
241	        workspace_root=WORKSPACE,
242	        files=files,
243	        sandbox_id=ws.sandbox_id,
244	    )
245	
246	
247	# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- observe
248	
249	
250	def faults_fired(ep: dict[str, Any]) -> list[FaultFired]:
251	    """Only what already happened. Pending faults stay in the Dict, invisible to everyone."""
252	    out: list[FaultFired] = []
253	    for entry in ep.get("ledger", []):
254	        f = entry.get("fault")
255	        if f:
256	            out.append(FaultFired.model_validate(f))
257	    return out
258	
259	
260	def observe(episode_id: str, ws: Workspace | None = None) -> ObserveResponse:
261	    ep = load(episode_id)
262	    baseline: dict[str, str] = ep.get("baseline", {})
263	    if ep.get("done") and ep.get("terminated"):
264	        # sandbox is gone; report the last known tree without touching Modal
265	        return ObserveResponse(
266	            episode_id=episode_id,
267	            step=ep.get("step", 0),
268	            files=[FileEntry(path=p, size=0, sha256=s, status="unchanged") for p, s in sorted(baseline.items())],
269	            diffs=[],
270	            faults_fired=faults_fired(ep),
271	            done=True,
272	        )
273	    ws = ws or workspace_for(ep)
274	    cur = ws.sha_map(WORKSPACE)
275	
276	    files: list[FileEntry] = []
277	    changed: list[str] = []
278	    for rel in sorted(set(cur) | set(baseline)):
279	        meta = cur.get(rel)
280	        base_sha = baseline.get(rel)
281	        if meta is None:
282	            files.append(FileEntry(path=rel, size=0, sha256=base_sha or "", status="deleted"))
283	            changed.append(rel)
284	        elif base_sha is None:
285	            files.append(FileEntry(path=rel, size=int(meta.get("size", 0)), sha256=meta["sha256"], status="added"))
286	            changed.append(rel)
287	        elif meta["sha256"] != base_sha:
288	            files.append(FileEntry(path=rel, size=int(meta.get("size", 0)), sha256=meta["sha256"], status="modified"))
289	            changed.append(rel)
290	        else:
291	            files.append(FileEntry(path=rel, size=int(meta.get("size", 0)), sha256=meta["sha256"], status="unchanged"))
292	
293	    diffs = [FileDiff(**d) for d in ws.diffs(changed, DIFF_CAP)] if changed else []
294	    log.info(
295	        "episode.observe",
296	        "workspace observed",
297	        episode_id=episode_id,
298	        step=ep.get("step", 0),
299	        files=len(files),
300	        changed=len(changed),
301	        faults_fired=len(faults_fired(ep)),
302	    )
303	    return ObserveResponse(
304	        episode_id=episode_id,
305	        step=ep.get("step", 0),
306	        files=files,
307	        diffs=diffs,
308	        faults_fired=faults_fired(ep),
309	        done=bool(ep.get("done", False)),
310	    )
311

316	def append_ledger(ep: dict[str, Any], entry: LedgerEntry) -> None:
317	    ep.setdefault("ledger", []).append(entry.model_dump())
318	
319	
320	def ledger_entries(ep: dict[str, Any]) -> list[LedgerEntry]:
321	    return [LedgerEntry.model_validate(e) for e in ep.get("ledger", [])]
322	
323	
324	def fault_plan_of(ep: dict[str, Any]) -> FaultPlan:
325	    return FaultPlan.model_validate(ep.get("fault_plan") or {})
326	
327	
328	# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- interruptions
329	#
330	# The harness is the only component that knows it *stopped listening*. When its worker is killed or
331	# its HTTP request is aborted mid-call, sandbox-env happily finishes the work and writes an `ok` row
332	# — which is the truth about the sandbox and a lie about the episode, because the agent never
333	# received that answer and has to treat the write as "may or may not have landed". `POST
334	# /episodes/{id}/interruptions` is how the harness tells us, and `interrupted: true` is how the
335	# grader learns to apply the `ack_lost` rule (GRADING.md `verified_before_rewrite`) to a real failure.
336	
337	
338	def _row_touches(entry: dict[str, Any], path: str) -> bool:
339	    """GRADING.md `touches`, against a raw ledger row."""
340	    if entry.get("tool") == "run_command":
341	        return faults.touches(path, "run_command", {"command": entry.get("command") or ""})
342	    return faults.normalize_token(entry.get("path") or "") == faults.normalize_token(path)
343	
344	
345	def match_interrupted(rows: list[dict[str, Any]], report: InterruptionReport) -> int | None:
346	    """Index of the most recent row the report could be about, or None.
347	
348	    `outcome in {ok, ack_lost}` is the whole point: those are the calls whose side effect really
349	    happened. A row that already failed at the boundary (`short_circuit`) or in the sandbox
350	    (`error`) needs no annotation — the agent got a definite answer for it.
351	
352	    Already-`interrupted` rows are eligible on purpose, so a harness that retries the report is
353	    idempotent instead of walking backwards and marking a second, unrelated call.
354	    """
355	    for i in range(len(rows) - 1, -1, -1):
356	        row_ = rows[i]
357	        if row_.get("tool") != report.tool:
358	            continue
359	        if row_.get("outcome") not in ("ok", "ack_lost"):
360	            continue
361	        if report.args_digest:
362	            if row_.get("args_digest") != report.args_digest:
363	                continue
364	        elif report.path:
365	            if not _row_touches(row_, report.path):
366	                continue
367	        return i
368	    return None
369	
370	
371	def report_interruption(episode_id: str, report: InterruptionReport) -> tuple[LedgerEntry, bool]:
372	    """Mark the call the harness never heard back from. Returns (row, matched).
373	
374	    When nothing matches, the call never reached this service at all (killed in the client, or the
375	    connection died before the request landed). That is still a fact about the episode, so it is
376	    recorded as an annotation row — `outcome: error`, `origin: real`, `interrupted: true` — rather
377	    than dropped, otherwise the evidence and the UI would show a step with no trace whatsoever.
378	    """
379	    ep = load(episode_id)
380	    rows: list[dict[str, Any]] = ep.setdefault("ledger", [])
381	    idx = match_interrupted(rows, report)
382	
383	    if idx is not None:
384	        rows[idx]["interrupted"] = True
385	        save(ep)
386	        entry = LedgerEntry.model_validate(rows[idx])
387	        log.warn(
388	            "episode.interrupted",
389	            f"{report.tool} at step {entry.step} was interrupted ({report.code})",
390	            episode_id=episode_id, tool=report.tool, path=report.path, step=entry.step,
391	            outcome=entry.outcome, code=report.code, layer=report.layer, matched=True,
392	        )
393	        return entry, True
394	
395	    entry = LedgerEntry(
396	        step=int(ep.get("step", 0)),
397	        ts=now_iso(),
398	        tool=report.tool,
399	        args_digest=report.args_digest
400	        or args_digest({"tool": report.tool, "path": report.path, "at": report.at}),
401	        path=report.path,
402	        command=None,
403	        mutating=faults.is_mutating(report.tool, {"path": report.path or ""}),
404	        fault=None,
405	        outcome="error",
406	        exit_code=None,
407	        duration_ms=0,
408	        origin="real",
409	        error_code=report.code,
410	        interrupted=True,
411	    )
412	    append_ledger(ep, entry)
413	    save(ep)
414	    log.warn(
415	        "episode.interrupted",
416	        f"{report.tool} never reached the sandbox ({report.code}); annotated the ledger",
417	        episode_id=episode_id, tool=report.tool, path=report.path, step=entry.step,
418	        code=report.code, layer=report.layer, matched=False,
419	    )
420	    return entry, False
421	
422	
423	# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- delete
424	
425	
426	def _terminate_quietly(ws: Workspace, episode_id: str, why: str) -> None:
427	    """Best-effort terminate on an error path. Never raises: the original error must win."""
428	    sandbox_id = getattr(ws, "sandbox_id", "?")
429	    try:
430	        ws.terminate()
431	        log.warn("episode.abandoned", why, episode_id=episode_id, sandbox_id=sandbox_id,
432	                 terminated=True)
433	    except BaseException as exc:  # noqa: BLE001
434	        log.error("episode.abandon_failed", f"{why}; terminate also failed: {exc}",
435	                  episode_id=episode_id, sandbox_id=sandbox_id, terminated=False)
436	
437	
438	def delete(episode_id: str) -> bool:
439	    """Terminate the sandbox and mark the episode done. Idempotent."""
440	    try:
441	        ep = load(episode_id)
442	    except EpisodeNotFound:
443	        return False
444	    if not ep.get("terminated"):
445	        try:
446	            workspace_for(ep).terminate()
447	        except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001 - a dead sandbox is still a successful delete
448	            log.warn("episode.terminate_failed", str(exc), episode_id=episode_id, sandbox_id=ep.get("sandbox_id"))
449	    ep["done"] = True
450	    ep["terminated"] = True
451	    ep["finished_at"] = now_iso()
452	    save(ep)
453	    log.info(
454	        "episode.deleted",
455	        "sandbox terminated",
456	        episode_id=episode_id,
457	        sandbox_id=ep.get("sandbox_id"),
458	        step=ep.get("step", 0),
459	    )
460	    return True
461	
462	
463	# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- lifecycle / ops
464	#
465	# Everything below is cost control. A Modal Sandbox costs money for as long as it runs, and the
466	# thing that keeps it alive — an episode nobody will ever DELETE — is invisible unless we look.
467	
468	
469	def age_s(ep: dict[str, Any], now: float | None = None) -> float:
470	    """Seconds since reset. An unparsable/missing `created_at` reads as 0 (never expire it)."""
471	    raw = ep.get("created_at")
472	    if not raw:
473	        return 0.0
474	    try:
475	        ts = datetime.fromisoformat(str(raw).replace("Z", "+00:00"))
476	    except ValueError:
477	        return 0.0
478	    if ts.tzinfo is None:
479	        ts = ts.replace(tzinfo=timezone.utc)
480	    now = now if now is not None else datetime.now(timezone.utc).timestamp()
481	    return max(0.0, now - ts.timestamp())
482	
483	
484	def episode_ids(limit: int = EPISODE_SWEEP_MAX) -> list[str]:
485	    """Ids currently in the store, newest-first is not guaranteed by modal.Dict — order is its own."""
486	    try:
487	        keys = list(store().keys())
488	    except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001 - an ops listing must never take the service down
489	        log.warn("episode.keys_failed", str(exc))
490	        return []
491	    return keys[:limit] if limit and limit > 0 else keys
492	
493	
494	def iter_episodes(limit: int = EPISODE_SWEEP_MAX) -> list[tuple[str, dict[str, Any]]]:
495	    """Every episode record, in as few round trips as the store allows.
496	
497	    `modal.Dict.items()` streams the whole Dict in ONE call; `keys()` + `get()` per id costs an
498	    RPC each (~50 ms), which turned a 125-episode Dict into a 6 s scan on the reset path — measured,
499	    not guessed (runs/20260912T230019Z_perf: reset p50 6.7 s before this, 1.9 s after).
500	    """
501	    st = store()
502	    items = getattr(st, "items", None)
503	    if callable(items):
504	        try:
505	            out: list[tuple[str, dict[str, Any]]] = []
506	            for key, value in items():
507	                if isinstance(value, dict):
508	                    out.append((str(key), value))
509	                if limit and len(out) >= limit:
510	                    break
511	            return out
512	        except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001 - fall back to the slow path rather than fail
513	            log.warn("episode.items_failed", str(exc))
514	    pairs: list[tuple[str, dict[str, Any]]] = []
515	    for eid in episode_ids(limit):
516	        ep = st.get(eid)
517	        if isinstance(ep, dict):
518	            pairs.append((eid, ep))
519	    return pairs
520	
521	
522	def row(ep: dict[str, Any], *, probe: bool = False, now: float | None = None) -> dict[str, Any]:
523	    """One ops row: who it is, how old, and whether its sandbox is still burning money.
524	
525	    `alive` is None when we did not look. A terminated episode answers False without a probe —
526	    the whole point of recording `terminated` is to avoid an RPC per row on the common case.
527	    """
528	    terminated = bool(ep.get("terminated"))
529	    alive: bool | None = False if terminated else None
530	    if probe and not terminated:
531	        try:
532	            alive = workspace_for(ep).alive()
533	        except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001
534	            log.debug("episode.probe_failed", str(exc), episode_id=ep.get("episode_id"))
535	            alive = False
536	    return {
537	        "episode_id": ep.get("episode_id"),
538	        "scenario_id": ep.get("scenario_id"),
539	        "created_at": ep.get("created_at"),
540	        "age_s": round(age_s(ep, now), 1),
541	        "step": ep.get("step", 0),
542	        "sandbox_id": ep.get("sandbox_id"),
543	        "alive": alive,
544	        "terminated": terminated,
545	        "done": bool(ep.get("done")),
546	        "score": ep.get("score"),
547	        "finished_at": ep.get("finished_at"),
548	    }
549	
550	
551	def list_episodes(probe: bool = True, limit: int = EPISODE_SWEEP_MAX) -> dict[str, Any]:
552	    """Ops listing for `GET /episodes`: id, scenario, created_at, sandbox alive?"""
553	    now = datetime.now(timezone.utc).timestamp()
554	    rows = [row(ep, probe=probe, now=now) for _eid, ep in iter_episodes(limit)]
555	    rows.sort(key=lambda r: r.get("created_at") or "", reverse=True)
556	    live = sum(1 for r in rows if r["alive"] is True)
557	    return {
558	        "count": len(rows),
559	        "live": live,
560	        "probed": probe,
561	        "ttl_s": EPISODE_TTL_S,
562	        "purge_s": EPISODE_PURGE_S,
563	        "episodes": rows,
564	    }
565	
566	
567	def sweep(ttl_s: int | None = None, purge_s: int | None = None,
568	          limit: int = EPISODE_SWEEP_MAX) -> dict[str, Any]:
569	    """Terminate episodes older than the TTL; drop long-dead records from the Dict.
570	
571	    Deliberately cheap and idempotent: it is called on the reset path, so it must cost at most a
572	    Dict scan plus one terminate per actually-expired episode, and it must never raise.
573	    """
574	    ttl = EPISODE_TTL_S if ttl_s is None else ttl_s
575	    purge = EPISODE_PURGE_S if purge_s is None else purge_s
576	    now = datetime.now(timezone.utc).timestamp()
577	    expired: list[str] = []
578	    purged: list[str] = []
579	    errors: list[str] = []
580	    scanned = 0
581	
582	    for eid, ep in iter_episodes(limit):
583	        try:
584	            scanned += 1
585	            age = age_s(ep, now)
586	            if not ep.get("terminated") and ttl > 0 and age > ttl:
587	                if delete(eid):
588	                    expired.append(eid)
589	            elif ep.get("terminated") and purge > 0 and age > purge:
590	                store().pop(eid, None)
591	                purged.append(eid)
592	        except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001 - one bad episode must not stop the sweep
593	            errors.append(f"{eid}: {type(exc).__name__}: {exc}")
594	
595	    if expired or purged or errors:
596	        log.info(
597	            "episode.sweep",
598	            f"expired {len(expired)}, purged {len(purged)}",
599	            scanned=scanned, ttl_s=ttl, purge_s=purge,
600	            expired=expired or None, purged=purged or None, errors=errors or None,
601	        )
602	    return {"scanned": scanned, "ttl_s": ttl, "purge_s": purge,
603	            "expired": expired, "purged": purged, "errors": errors}
604	
605	
606	#: wall-clock of this container's last sweep (monotonic). Per container on purpose: the sweep is
607	#: idempotent, so N containers sweeping independently is harmless, and a shared lock in the Dict
608	#: would cost the very round trip the throttle exists to avoid.
609	_last_sweep_at: float = 0.0
610	
611	
612	def reset_sweep_throttle() -> None:
613	    """Forget the last sweep time (tests, and the out-of-band `sweep` Modal function)."""
614	    global _last_sweep_at
615	    _last_sweep_at = 0.0
616	
617	
618	def sweep_quietly(force: bool = False) -> dict[str, Any]:
619	    """`sweep()` on the reset path: throttled, and unable to break its caller.
620	
621	    Throttled because reset is the latency-critical call in the system (the browser is watching)
622	    and the sweep is pure housekeeping: paying for it on *every* reset put ~4.5 s on the p50 with a
623	    125-episode Dict. Once per EPISODE_SWEEP_INTERVAL_S per container is plenty — an abandoned
624	    sandbox is reclaimed within the interval, and the sandbox's own idle_timeout is the backstop.
625	    """
626	    global _last_sweep_at
627	    now = time.monotonic()
628	    if not force and _last_sweep_at and (now - _last_sweep_at) < EPISODE_SWEEP_INTERVAL_S:
629	        return {"scanned": 0, "expired": [], "purged": [], "errors": [], "skipped": "throttled"}
630	    _last_sweep_at = now
631	    try:
632	        return sweep()
633	    except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001
634	        log.warn("episode.sweep_failed", str(exc))
635	        return {"scanned": 0, "expired": [], "purged": [], "errors": [str(exc)]}
636	
637	
638	def active_sandboxes(ttl_s: int | None = None, limit: int = EPISODE_SWEEP_MAX) -> dict[str, dict[str, Any]]:
639	    """`{sandbox_id: {episode_id, scenario_id, age_s, step}}` for episodes still in use.
640	
641	    "In use" == not terminated, not done, and younger than the TTL. This is the set `reap` must not
642	    touch: killing one of these is exactly what ended run `r_ccda8780cbee` at step 4 with a
643	    mislabelled EINTERNAL and a null score.
644	    """
645	    ttl = EPISODE_TTL_S if ttl_s is None else ttl_s
646	    now = datetime.now(timezone.utc).timestamp()
647	    out: dict[str, dict[str, Any]] = {}
648	    for _eid, ep in iter_episodes(limit):
649	        if ep.get("terminated") or ep.get("done"):
650	            continue
651	        age = age_s(ep, now)
652	        if ttl <= 0 or age <= ttl:
653	            sid = ep.get("sandbox_id")
654	            if sid:
655	                out[str(sid)] = {
656	                    "episode_id": ep.get("episode_id"),
657	                    "scenario_id": ep.get("scenario_id"),
658	                    "age_s": round(age, 1),
659	                    "step": ep.get("step", 0),
660	                }
661	    return out
662	
663	
664	def active_sandbox_ids(ttl_s: int | None = None, limit: int = EPISODE_SWEEP_MAX) -> set[str]:
665	    """Sandbox ids belonging to episodes that are still in use (see `active_sandboxes`)."""
666	    return set(active_sandboxes(ttl_s=ttl_s, limit=limit))
667	
668	
669	def mark_terminated(sandbox_ids: set[str], reason: str = "reaped",
670	                    limit: int = EPISODE_SWEEP_MAX) -> list[str]:
671	    """Record that these sandboxes are gone, so `GET /episodes` stops claiming they are alive."""
672	    touched: list[str] = []
673	    if not sandbox_ids:
674	        return touched
675	    for eid, ep in iter_episodes(limit):
676	        if ep.get("terminated"):
677	            continue
678	        if str(ep.get("sandbox_id")) in sandbox_ids:
679	            ep["terminated"] = True
680	            ep["done"] = True
681	            ep["finished_at"] = now_iso()
682	            ep["terminated_by"] = reason
683	            save(ep)
684	            touched.append(eid)
685	    if touched:
686	        log.info("episode.marked_terminated", f"{len(touched)} episode(s) {reason}",
687	                 episodes=touched, reason=reason)
688	    return touched
